| DIS | ключ кортежа | 1 094 | 38 000 | `204fa98695f025e3cc25bf8dcb2ad74e628e87a34ebb43bfaeefac8e3bf03605` |
| **X_DEC** | ключ кортежа | 3 536 | 125 646 | `9e05b2663526c760298a5b575440e6c8c88d5b95448a3cdebb8554a59c30d865` |
| X (CENSUS) | = Π (без меток) | 11 765 | 413 959 | `11606a21fdcadab1b197d6eda7b681be776e6e456e36f1b9e8ae77ace9c4e115` |
| REQ (обязательные ячейки, DECISION) | `S\|d\|caseId\|sideId\|population\|examined` | 452 | 16 757 | `712cfcc7faa55f5e1525da0b7dc6792ad05714cca38a56b08bb3c052427f035e` |
| UNR (необязательные ячейки, DECISION) | `S\|d\|caseId\|sideId\|population` | 123 | 4 260 | `71fe7326138993ddc5c1050ccf5a79c5c3da895572a47e8e0ffbd795d0fade42` |
| E-MX (выдержка METHOD, §7.4) | строки METHOD, без сортировки, в порядке §7.4 | 206 | 32 450 | `158cff914667ba2cd515c97d116628aaacd92505a3b26fc54d9a9ede05478d90` |
| QB (банк QUAL, Annex F) | канонические JSON-строки в порядке Q-01…Q-10, без сортировки | 10 | 1 757 | `89d08fb83a167f826fcf75dbab03fc5c814951cd51da0b1fe8efe6dbc8f2f3f3` |
| BB (байты блока брифа Annex B v2, §11.4) ⟦C1-F01⟧ | строки блока, без сортировки, каждая с LF | 94 | 5 596 | `d76acdd31b61ebd8be66980a0d774b85ffc45713a14de45faafa344af3aa88c1` |

Ранг — 0-базовая позиция факта по возрастанию `SHA-256(UTF-8(caseId + "|" + sideId + "|" + factId))` (нижний регистр hex). Правила E-6 и E-7 — §5.1 (из S^F и рангов). Δ, K1, DIS, X_DEC — §5.3. REQ/UNR — §5.4. E-4 (B-CORE) не используется (§9.9).

## ANNEX F — БАНК QUAL (фиксирован; дословные байты строк блока ниже, каждая с LF = QB)

Синтетические контроли о вымышленных организациях. Построенные выходы — константы дизайна контроля, а не эталонные метки. YES-факты касаются каждой части своего вопроса; NO-факты не касаются ни одной.

```text
{"d":"D-01","factText":"Velmora Textiles publishes a charter on its public website stating that the company exists to make durable clothing affordable for working families.","output":"YES","qualId":"Q-01"}
{"d":"D-03","factText":"At Brannock Mutual, branch staff are promoted strictly by length of service, and the personnel office applies the seniority list to every promotion.","output":"YES","qualId":"Q-02"}
{"d":"D-04","factText":"At Tessel Logistics, the founder alone approves every route change; the regional managers pass her decisions to depot staff and check each week that the decisions are carried out.","output":"YES","qualId":"Q-03"}
{"d":"D-05","factText":"At Orrin Bakeries, every loaf is mixed, shaped and baked by hand by a two-person team that follows the same written sequence each night.","output":"YES","qualId":"Q-04"}
{"d":"D-09","factText":"At the Quillfield Co-op, every withdrawal from the equipment fund must be repaid from the sales of the following season, and the treasurer reports each repayment to the meeting of members.","output":"YES","qualId":"Q-05"}
{"d":"D-02","factText":"The head office of Harlow Instruments is a brick building beside the river.","output":"NO","qualId":"Q-06"}
{"d":"D-06","factText":"Mistral Ceramics sells its dinner plates in three sizes.","output":"NO","qualId":"Q-07"}
{"d":"D-07","factText":"The office of Penrose Analytics is on the third floor of a shared building.","output":"NO","qualId":"Q-08"}
{"d":"D-10","factText":"The annual report of Corvel Shipping lists the registered address of the company and the name of its auditor.","output":"NO","qualId":"Q-09"}
{"d":"D-13","factText":"The website of the Ardent Paper Company uses a dark blue background.","output":"NO","qualId":"Q-10"}
```

Поле `output` в пакет QUAL не входит: пакет — ровно семь ключей C.1Q.

## ANNEX G — ИСПОЛНЯЕМЫЙ КОД ОЦЕНКИ (нормативная реализация; Python 3, только стандартная библиотека)

Код между маркерами — нормативный блок. Его SHA-256 (байтов строк между строкой ```` ```python ```` и закрывающей строкой ```` ``` ````, с LF) — предмет EG-10.

- Функции до комментария `worked examples` нормативны.
- Функции и данные после него — примеры §6.8, §8.11 и контрпримеры §16.2; на решение исполнения они не влияют.
- `run_all()` возвращает сводку и `True` ровно при совпадении всех ожидаемых значений.
- Код не обращается к файлам, сети и времени.

<!-- SI0CR1-ANNEX-G-CODE-BEGIN -->
```python
# SI0CR1 ANNEX G v3 :: normative evaluation code of SI-0 COMPACT v1.0 CORR2
import hashlib
import json
from itertools import product

YES = "YES"
NO = "NO"
SUR = "SURVIVED_WITHIN_TESTED_BOUND"
FIRED = "FIRED"
NT = "NOT_TESTED"
ND = "NOT_DETERMINABLE"
SUFF = "SCIENTIFICALLY_SUFFICIENT_WITHIN_BOUND"
INSUFF = "SCIENTIFICALLY_INSUFFICIENT"
NOT_EST = "NOT_ESTABLISHED"
LABELS = ("Y", "N", "I", "U-NR", "U-ND", "U-NX")
DS = tuple("D-%02d" % i for i in range(1, 14))
PART_DELIMS = (",", ";", "?")
STRATA = ("YY", "NN", "YN", "NY")
QUAL_CASE = "SYNTHETIC-QUAL"
QUAL_SIDE = "QUAL"
ND_NOTE = "reviewers could not determine"
READING_KEYS = {"readingId", "output", "questionAnchor", "factAnchor", "gloss", "status", "exclusion"}
FX_KEYS = {"flipClass", "flipOutput", "questionAnchor", "criterion", "quote"}
BLIND_KEYS = {"packetId", "readings", "flipExclusions", "state", "output", "constructNote"}
PKT_KEYS = {"packetId", "caseId", "sideId", "factId", "factText", "d", "question"}
CR_KEYS = {"packetId", "verdict", "endorsements", "challenges"}
CH_KEYS = {"basis", "target", "reading"}
CRD_KEYS = {"output", "questionAnchor", "factAnchor", "gloss"}
CHAL_ITEM_KEYS = {"packetId", "packet", "ownRecord", "otherRecord", "sharedOutput"}
H_ITEM_KEYS = {"factKey", "factText", "domains"}
H_DOMAIN_KEYS = {"d", "question", "readings"}
H_REC_KEYS = {"factKey", "determination", "factAnchors", "contractAnchors", "gloss"}
H_DET = ("INVARIANT_PRESENT", "INVARIANT_ABSENT", "VARIES", "CANNOT_DETERMINE")
H_ROUTES = ("IN_PROTOCOL", "EXTERNAL")


def opposite(o):
    return NO if o == YES else YES


def canon(obj):
    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))


def pid(phase, slot, key):
    return hashlib.sha256(("SI0CR1|" + phase + "|" + slot + "|" + key).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:32]


def tuple_key(p):
    return "|".join((p["caseId"], p["sideId"], p["factId"], p["d"]))


def fact_of(t):
    return t.rsplit("|", 1)[0]


def _verbatim(s, *texts):
    return isinstance(s, str) and len(s) > 0 and any(s in t for t in texts)


def question_parts(q):
    spans, a = [], 0
    for i, ch in enumerate(q):
        if ch in PART_DELIMS:
            if any(c.isalnum() for c in q[a:i]):
                spans.append((a, i))
            a = i + 1
    if any(c.isalnum() for c in q[a:]):
        spans.append((a, len(q)))
    return spans


def _occurrences(s, q):
    out, i = [], q.find(s)
    while i >= 0:
        out.append((i, i + len(s)))
        i = q.find(s, i + 1)
    return out


def parts_addressed(rec, q):
    anchors = [r["questionAnchor"] for r in rec["readings"]] + [e["questionAnchor"] for e in rec["flipExclusions"]]
    for a, b in question_parts(q):
        hit = False
        for an in anchors:
            for x, y in _occurrences(an, q):
                lo, hi = max(a, x), min(b, y)
                if lo < hi and any(c.isalnum() for c in q[lo:hi]):
                    hit = True
        if not hit:
            return False
    return True


def derived_state(rec, q):
    rs = rec["readings"]
    adm = {r["output"] for r in rs if r["status"] == "ADMISSIBLE"}
    nex = {r["output"] for r in rs if r["status"] == "NOT_EXCLUDED"}
    if adm == {YES, NO}:
        return ("S1", None)
    if len(adm) == 1:
        a = next(iter(adm))
        opp = [e for e in rec["flipExclusions"] if e["flipOutput"] != a]
        if (nex - adm) or not opp or not parts_addressed(rec, q):
            return ("S3", None)
        return ("S2", a)
    return ("ND", None)


def validate_blind(rec, pkt):
    if not isinstance(rec, dict) or set(rec) != BLIND_KEYS:
        return (False, "keys")
    if rec["packetId"] != pkt["packetId"]:
        return (False, "packetId")
    q, t = pkt["question"], pkt["factText"]
    rs = rec["readings"]
    if not isinstance(rs, list) or not (1 <= len(rs) <= 6):
        return (False, "readings")
    seen = set()
    for r in rs:
        if not isinstance(r, dict) or set(r) != READING_KEYS:
            return (False, "readingKeys")
        if not isinstance(r["readingId"], str) or not r["readingId"] or r["readingId"] in seen:
            return (False, "readingId")
        seen.add(r["readingId"])
        if r["output"] not in (YES, NO):
            return (False, "output")
        if r["status"] not in ("ADMISSIBLE", "NOT_EXCLUDED", "EXCLUDED"):
            return (False, "status")
        if not _verbatim(r["questionAnchor"], q):
            return (False, "questionAnchor")
        fa = r["factAnchor"]
        if not isinstance(fa, str) or (fa and fa not in t):
            return (False, "factAnchor")
        if r["output"] == YES and r["status"] != "EXCLUDED" and not fa:
            return (False, "yesSupport")
        if not isinstance(r["gloss"], str) or len(r["gloss"]) > 300:
            return (False, "gloss")
        ex = r["exclusion"]
        if r["status"] == "EXCLUDED":
            if not isinstance(ex, dict) or set(ex) != {"criterion", "quote"}:
                return (False, "exclusion")
            if ex["criterion"] not in ("a", "b", "c") or not _verbatim(ex["quote"], q, t):
                return (False, "exclusion")
        elif ex is not None:
            return (False, "exclusion")
    fx = rec["flipExclusions"]
    if not isinstance(fx, list) or len(fx) > 6:
        return (False, "flipExclusions")
    for e in fx:
        if not isinstance(e, dict) or set(e) != FX_KEYS:
            return (False, "flipExclusion")
        if not isinstance(e["flipClass"], str) or not (1 <= len(e["flipClass"]) <= 200):
            return (False, "flipExclusion")
        if e["flipOutput"] not in (YES, NO) or not _verbatim(e["questionAnchor"], q):
            return (False, "flipExclusion")
        if e["criterion"] not in ("a", "b", "c") or not _verbatim(e["quote"], q, t):
            return (False, "flipExclusion")
    cn = rec["constructNote"]
    if cn is not None and (not isinstance(cn, str) or len(cn) > 300):
        return (False, "constructNote")
    if (rec["state"], rec["output"]) != derived_state(rec, q):
        return (False, "stateOutput")
    return (True, "")


def excl_ids(rec, side):
    ids = [side + ":fx:%d" % i for i in range(len(rec["flipExclusions"]))]
    return ids + [side + ":rd:" + r["readingId"] for r in rec["readings"] if r["status"] == "EXCLUDED"]


def validate_chal(cr, pkt, own, other, shared):
    if not isinstance(cr, dict) or set(cr) != CR_KEYS or cr["packetId"] != pkt["packetId"]:
        return (False, "keys")
    q, t = pkt["question"], pkt["factText"]
    oth = excl_ids(other, "other")
    allx = set(oth) | set(excl_ids(own, "own"))
    en, ch = cr["endorsements"], cr["challenges"]
    if not isinstance(en, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in en):
        return (False, "endorsements")
    if len(set(en)) != len(en) or not set(en) <= set(oth):
        return (False, "endorsements")
    if not isinstance(ch, list):
        return (False, "challenges")
    if cr["verdict"] == "CONFIRM":
        if ch or set(en) != set(oth):
            return (False, "confirm")
        return (True, "")
    if cr["verdict"] != "CHALLENGE" or not (1 <= len(ch) <= 6):
        return (False, "verdict")
    for c in ch:
        if not isinstance(c, dict) or set(c) != CH_KEYS:
            return (False, "challengeKeys")
        if c["basis"] == "WRONG_EXCLUSION":
            if c["target"] not in allx:
                return (False, "target")
        elif c["basis"] == "UNWRITTEN_CLASS":
            if c["target"] is not None:
                return (False, "target")
        else:
            return (False, "basis")
        r = c["reading"]
        if not isinstance(r, dict) or set(r) != CRD_KEYS:
            return (False, "readingKeys")
        if r["output"] != opposite(shared):
            return (False, "output")
        if not _verbatim(r["questionAnchor"], q):
            return (False, "questionAnchor")
        fa = r["factAnchor"]
        if not isinstance(fa, str) or (fa and fa not in t) or (r["output"] == YES and not fa):
            return (False, "factAnchor")
        if not isinstance(r["gloss"], str) or len(r["gloss"]) > 300:
            return (False, "gloss")
    return (True, "")


def strip_pid(rec):
    return {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k != "packetId"}


def validate_packet_main(p, ctx):
    if not isinstance(p, dict) or set(p) != PKT_KEYS:
        return (False, "keys")
    if p["d"] not in DS or p["question"] != ctx["questions"].get(p["d"]):
        return (False, "question")
    fk = "|".join((p["caseId"], p["sideId"], p["factId"]))
    if fk not in ctx["stage1"] or p["factText"] != ctx["stage1"][fk]:
        return (False, "stage1")
    if p["packetId"] != pid("MAIN", ctx["slot"], tuple_key(p)):
        return (False, "packetId")
    return (True, "")


def validate_packet_qual(p, ctx):
    if not isinstance(p, dict) or set(p) != PKT_KEYS:
        return (False, "keys")
    if p["caseId"] != QUAL_CASE or p["sideId"] != QUAL_SIDE:
        return (False, "synthetic")
    b = ctx["bank"].get(p["factId"])
    if b is None or p["factText"] != b["factText"] or p["d"] != b["d"]:
        return (False, "bank")
    if p["question"] != ctx["questions"].get(p["d"]):
        return (False, "question")
    if p["packetId"] != pid("QUAL", ctx["slot"], tuple_key(p)):
        return (False, "packetId")
    return (True, "")


def validate_item_chal(it, ctx):
    if not isinstance(it, dict) or set(it) != CHAL_ITEM_KEYS:
        return (False, "keys")
    s = ctx["slot"]
    o = "R2" if s == "R1" else "R1"
    p = it["packet"]
    if not validate_packet_main(p, ctx)[0] or it["packetId"] != p["packetId"]:
        return (False, "packet")
    k = tuple_key(p)
    own, oth = ctx["blind"].get((s, k)), ctx["blind"].get((o, k))
    if own is None or oth is None or it["ownRecord"] != own or it["otherRecord"] != strip_pid(oth):
        return (False, "records")
    po = dict(p)
    po["packetId"] = oth["packetId"]
    if not validate_blind(own, p)[0] or not validate_blind(oth, po)[0]:
        return (False, "blind")
    so, oo = derived_state(own, p["question"])
    st, ot = derived_state(oth, p["question"])
    if not (so == "S2" and st == "S2" and oo == ot == it["sharedOutput"]):
        return (False, "notCandidate")
    return (True, "")


def validate_item_h(it, ctx):
    if not isinstance(it, dict) or set(it) != H_ITEM_KEYS:
        return (False, "keys")
    fk = it["factKey"]
    if fk not in ctx["rh"] or it["factText"] != ctx["stage1"].get(fk):
        return (False, "fact")
    ds = it["domains"]
    if not isinstance(ds, list) or not all(isinstance(x, dict) and set(x) == H_DOMAIN_KEYS for x in ds):
        return (False, "domains")
    if [x["d"] for x in ds] != sorted(ctx["rh"][fk]):
        return (False, "domains")
    for x in ds:
        if x["question"] != ctx["questions"].get(x["d"]):
            return (False, "question")
        if x["readings"] != ctx["recorded"].get(fk + "|" + x["d"]):
            return (False, "readings")
    return (True, "")


def validate_input(phase, obj, ctx):
    fn = {"MAIN": validate_packet_main, "QUAL": validate_packet_qual,
          "CHAL": validate_item_chal, "H": validate_item_h}.get(phase)
    if fn is None:
        return (False, "phase")
    try:
        return fn(obj, ctx)
    except (KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError):
        return (False, "shape")


def validate_h_record(rec, item, excerpt):
    if not isinstance(rec, dict) or set(rec) != H_REC_KEYS or rec["factKey"] != item["factKey"]:
        return (False, "keys")
    if rec["determination"] not in H_DET:
        return (False, "determination")
    fa, ca = rec["factAnchors"], rec["contractAnchors"]
    if not isinstance(fa, list) or not isinstance(ca, list) or len(fa) > 6 or len(ca) > 6:
        return (False, "anchors")
    if not all(_verbatim(x, item["factText"]) for x in fa) or not all(_verbatim(x, excerpt) for x in ca):
        return (False, "anchors")
    if rec["determination"] != "CANNOT_DETERMINE" and (not fa or not ca):
        return (False, "anchorsRequired")
    if not isinstance(rec["gloss"], str) or len(rec["gloss"]) > 600:
        return (False, "gloss")
    return (True, "")


def qual_pass(packets, records, bank):
    if len(packets) != len(bank) or len(records) != len(packets):
        return False
    for p, r in zip(packets, records):
        b = bank.get(p["factId"])
        if b is None or r is None or not validate_blind(r, p)[0]:
            return False
        if derived_state(r, p["question"]) != ("S2", b["output"]):
            return False
    return True


def assign_label(p1, p2, r1, r2, c1=None, c2=None):
    if r1 is None or r2 is None or not validate_blind(r1, p1)[0] or not validate_blind(r2, p2)[0]:
        return ("U-NX", "BLIND_INCOMPLETE")
    s1, o1 = derived_state(r1, p1["question"])
    s2, o2 = derived_state(r2, p2["question"])
    if s1 == "S2" and s2 == "S2" and o1 == o2:
        v1 = c1 is not None and validate_chal(c1, p1, r1, r2, o1)[0]
        v2 = c2 is not None and validate_chal(c2, p2, r2, r1, o1)[0]
        if (v1 and c1["verdict"] == "CHALLENGE") or (v2 and c2["verdict"] == "CHALLENGE"):
            return ("U-NR", "DETERMINACY_CHALLENGED")
        if v1 and v2:
            return ("Y" if o1 == YES else "N", "BLIND_S2_REPRODUCED_UNCHALLENGED")
        return ("U-NX", "CHALLENGE_INCOMPLETE")
    if s1 == "S1" and s2 == "S1":
        return ("I", "BLIND_S1_REPRODUCED")
    if s1 == "ND" or s2 == "ND":
        return ("U-ND", "NOT_DETERMINABLE_FROM_PACKET")
    return ("U-NR", "NOT_REPRODUCED")


def recorded_readings(label, r1, r2, c1=None, c2=None):
    if label == "U-ND":
        return ND_NOTE
    out = [{k: r[k] for k in ("output", "questionAnchor", "factAnchor", "gloss", "status")}
           for rec in (r1, r2) for r in rec["readings"]]
    for cr in (c1, c2):
        if cr is not None and cr.get("verdict") == "CHALLENGE":
            for c in cr["challenges"]:
                x = dict(c["reading"])
                x["status"] = "EXHIBITED_IN_CHALLENGE"
                out.append(x)
    return sorted(out, key=canon)


def clause_state(c, lab):
    vs = [lab[t] for t in c["tuples"]]
    if c["type"] == "LIT":
        if vs[0] in ("Y", "N"):
            return "SAT" if vs[0] == c["req"] else "VIOL"
        return "OPEN"
    if "Y" in vs:
        return "SAT"
    if all(v == "N" for v in vs):
        return "VIOL"
    return "OPEN"


def clause_value(c, comp):
    if c["type"] == "LIT":
        return comp[c["tuples"][0]] == (YES if c["req"] == "Y" else NO)
    return any(comp[t] == YES for t in c["tuples"])


def general_label_materiality(c, lab):
    if clause_state(c, lab) != "OPEN":
        return "NONE"
    det = {t: (YES if lab[t] == "Y" else NO) for t in c["tuples"] if lab[t] in ("Y", "N")}
    i_set = [t for t in c["tuples"] if lab[t] == "I"]
    u_set = [t for t in c["tuples"] if lab[t] in ("U-NR", "U-ND", "U-NX")]
    if not i_set:
        return "NONE"
    piv = []
    for uc in product((YES, NO), repeat=len(u_set)):
        vals = set()
        for ic in product((YES, NO), repeat=len(i_set)):
            comp = dict(det)
            comp.update(zip(u_set, uc))
            comp.update(zip(i_set, ic))
            vals.add(clause_value(c, comp))
        piv.append(len(vals) == 2)
    return "ESTABLISHED" if all(piv) else ("CONDITIONAL" if any(piv) else "NONE")


def label_materiality(c, lab):
    if clause_state(c, lab) != "OPEN":
        return "NONE"
    i_set = [t for t in c["tuples"] if lab[t] == "I"]
    u_set = [t for t in c["tuples"] if lab[t] in ("U-NR", "U-ND", "U-NX")]
    if not i_set:
        return "NONE"
    if c["type"] == "LIT":
        return "ESTABLISHED"
    return "CONDITIONAL" if u_set else "ESTABLISHED"


def ep1(clauses, lab):
    st = {c["id"]: clause_state(c, lab) for c in clauses}
    if any(v == "VIOL" for v in st.values()):
        return FIRED, st
    if all(v == "SAT" for v in st.values()):
        return SUR, st
    open_t = [t for c in clauses if st[c["id"]] == "OPEN" for t in c["tuples"]]
    return (NT if any(lab[t] == "U-NX" for t in open_t) else ND), st


def _lit_state(pop, lab):
    vs = [lab[t] for t in pop]
    if "Y" in vs:
        return FIRED
    if "U-NX" in vs:
        return NT
    if any(v in ("I", "U-NR", "U-ND") for v in vs):
        return ND
    return SUR


def fps01(clauses, lab, stratum):
    pop = [c["tuples"][0] for c in clauses
           if c["type"] == "LIT" and c["req"] == "N" and stratum[c["tuples"][0]] == "NN"]
    return _lit_state(pop, lab)


def fps02(clauses, lab, stratum, st):
    pop = [c["tuples"][0] for c in clauses
           if c["type"] == "LIT" and c["req"] == "N" and stratum[c["tuples"][0]] in ("YN", "NY")]
    a = _lit_state(pop, lab)
    fired, nt, nd = a == FIRED, a == NT, a == ND
    for c in clauses:
        if c["type"] != "FREE":
            continue
        att = [t for t in c["attr"] if stratum[t] in ("YN", "NY")]
        if not att or st[c["id"]] == "SAT":
            continue
        if st[c["id"]] == "VIOL" and any(lab[t] == "N" for t in att):
            fired = True
        elif any(lab[t] == "U-NX" for t in att + c["tuples"]):
            nt = True
        else:
            nd = True
    return FIRED if fired else (NT if nt else (ND if nd else SUR))


def coverage_map_complete(cells, xset):
    ds, sides, owner = set(), set(), {}
    for k, ts in cells.items():
        p = k.split("|") if isinstance(k, str) else []
        if len(p) != 4 or p[0] not in STRATA or not isinstance(ts, list):
            return False
        ds.add(p[1])
        sides.add(p[2] + "|" + p[3])
        for t in ts:
            q = t.split("|") if isinstance(t, str) else []
            if len(q) != 4 or q[3] != p[1] or q[0] + "|" + q[1] != p[2] + "|" + p[3] or t in owner:
                return False
            owner[t] = k
    for t in xset:
        q = t.split("|")
        if len(q) != 4:
            return False
        ds.add(q[3])
        sides.add(q[0] + "|" + q[1])
    if set(cells) != {s + "|" + d + "|" + sd for s in STRATA for d in ds for sd in sides}:
        return False
    return all(t in owner for t in xset)


def fps03(cells, xset, lab):
    if not cells or not coverage_map_complete(cells, xset):
        return NT, {}
    reg = {}
    for k, ts in cells.items():
        ex = [t for t in ts if t in xset]
        if not ts:
            state = "STRUCTURALLY_EMPTY"
        elif ex and all(lab[t] != "U-NX" for t in ex):
            state = "EVALUATED"
        else:
            state = "NOT_ESTABLISHED"
        reg[k] = {"state": state, "required": bool(ex), "population": len(ts), "examined": len(ex)}
    fired = any(v["required"] and v["state"] == "NOT_ESTABLISHED" for v in reg.values())
    return (FIRED if fired else SUR), reg


def r_h(xset, lab):
    return sorted({fact_of(t) for t in xset if lab[t] in ("I", "U-NR", "U-ND")})


def h21_state(a1, a2):
    if a1 is None or a2 is None:
        return NT
    if a1 == a2 and a1 in ("INVARIANT_PRESENT", "INVARIANT_ABSENT"):
        return "IMMATERIAL"
    if a1 == a2 == "VARIES":
        return "MATERIAL"
    return ND


def h_gate(e1, xset, lab):
    return bool(r_h(xset, lab)) and e1 == SUR and all(lab[t] != "U-NX" for t in xset)


def fps05(clauses, lab, xset, h21, h_run):
    lm = {c["id"]: label_materiality(c, lab) for c in clauses}
    hs = {f: (h21.get(f, NT) if h_run else ND) for f in r_h(xset, lab)}
    if any(v == "ESTABLISHED" for v in lm.values()) or any(v == "MATERIAL" for v in hs.values()):
        return FIRED, lm, hs
    if any(lab[t] == "U-NX" for t in xset) or any(v == NT for v in hs.values()):
        return NT, lm, hs
    if any(v == "CONDITIONAL" for v in lm.values()) or any(v != "IMMATERIAL" for v in hs.values()):
        return ND, lm, hs
    return SUR, lm, hs


def si6(f, pre):
    if pre["P1"] is not True or pre["P2"] is not True or pre["P5"] is not True or f["F-PS-07"] != SUR:
        return NOT_EST, 1
    if f["F-PS-04"] != SUR:
        return NOT_EST, 2
    if any(f[k] == FIRED for k in ("F-PS-01", "F-PS-02", "F-PS-05", "F-PS-06")):
        return INSUFF, 3
    if any(v in (NT, ND) for v in f.values()) or f["F-PS-03"] == FIRED or not all(pre[k] is True for k in ("P7", "P8", "P9")):
        return NOT_EST, 4
    return SUFF, 5


def evaluate(case):
    lab, xset, clauses = case["labels"], set(case["X"]), case["clauses"]
    if case["route"] not in H_ROUTES:
        raise ValueError("route")
    if set(lab) != xset or any(lab[t] not in LABELS for t in xset):
        raise ValueError("labels must be given for exactly the tuples of X")
    for c in clauses:
        if not all(t in xset for t in c["tuples"]):
            raise ValueError("clause outside X")
    e1, st = ep1(clauses, lab)
    gate = case["route"] == "IN_PROTOCOL" and h_gate(e1, xset, lab)
    f03, reg = fps03(case["cells"], xset, lab)
    f = {"F-PS-01": fps01(clauses, lab, case["stratum"]),
         "F-PS-02": fps02(clauses, lab, case["stratum"], st),
         "F-PS-03": f03,
         "F-PS-04": case["process"]["F-PS-04"],
         "F-PS-05": fps05(clauses, lab, xset, case["h21"] if gate else {}, gate)[0],
         "F-PS-06": e1,
         "F-PS-07": case["process"]["F-PS-07"]}
    out, step = si6(f, case["pre"])
    return {"EP-1": e1, "hGate": gate, "falsifiers": f, "coverage": reg, "SI-6": out, "step": step}

# ---- worked examples and verification fixtures (not normative) ----
TOY_D = ("D-01", "D-02", "D-03", "D-04", "D-05")
TOY_F = ("c|s|p", "c|s|q", "c|s|k", "c|t|e")
E = "c|t|e"
IMM = {"c|t|e": "IMMATERIAL"}
ABBR = {SUR: "S", FIRED: "F", NT: "NT", ND: "ND", SUFF: "SUFF", INSUFF: "INSUFF", NOT_EST: "NOT_EST"}


def toy_case(over, strat_over, route, h21, proc_over, pre_over, extra=()):
    xs = [f + "|" + d for f in TOY_F for d in TOY_D]
    lab = {t: "N" for t in xs}
    lab["c|s|p|D-01"] = "Y"
    lab["c|t|e|D-03"] = "Y"
    lab.update(over)
    stratum = {t: "NN" for t in xs}
    stratum["c|s|p|D-01"] = "YY"
    stratum["c|t|e|D-04"] = "YN"
    stratum.update(strat_over)
    stratum.update(dict(extra))
    clauses = [{"id": "LIT:c|s|p|D-01", "type": "LIT", "tuples": ["c|s|p|D-01"], "req": "Y"},
               {"id": "LIT:c|s|q|D-01", "type": "LIT", "tuples": ["c|s|q|D-01"], "req": "N"}]
    clauses += [{"id": "LIT:c|s|k|" + d, "type": "LIT", "tuples": ["c|s|k|" + d], "req": "N"} for d in TOY_D]
    clauses += [{"id": "LIT:c|t|e|" + d, "type": "LIT", "tuples": ["c|t|e|" + d], "req": "N"} for d in ("D-01", "D-02")]
    clauses.append({"id": "FREE:c|t|e", "type": "FREE", "tuples": ["c|t|e|D-03", "c|t|e|D-04", "c|t|e|D-05"],
                    "attr": [t for t in xs if t.startswith("c|t|e|") and stratum[t] != "NN"]})
    cells = {}
    for t in xs + [t for t, _ in extra]:
        c_, s_, f_, d_ = t.split("|")
        cells.setdefault(stratum[t] + "|" + d_ + "|" + c_ + "|" + s_, []).append(t)
    ds_ = {k.split("|")[1] for k in cells}
    sd_ = {k.split("|", 2)[2] for k in cells}
    for s_ in STRATA:
        for d_ in ds_:
            for x_ in sd_:
                cells.setdefault(s_ + "|" + d_ + "|" + x_, [])
    process = {"F-PS-04": SUR, "F-PS-07": SUR}
    process.update(proc_over)
    pre = {k: True for k in ("P1", "P2", "P5", "P7", "P8", "P9")}
    pre.update(pre_over)
    return {"labels": lab, "X": xs, "clauses": clauses, "stratum": stratum, "cells": cells,
            "route": route, "h21": h21, "process": process, "pre": pre}

    ("W2", {E + "|D-03": "N", E + "|D-04": "I"}, {}, "IN_PROTOCOL", IMM, {}, {}, (False, "ND", "S", "ND", "S", "F", "ND", "INSUFF")),

    ("W4", {E + "|D-03": "U-NR", E + "|D-04": "I"}, {}, "IN_PROTOCOL", IMM, {}, {}, (False, "ND", "S", "ND", "S", "ND", "ND", "NOT_EST")),

    ("W6", {E + "|D-03": "U-NX", E + "|D-04": "I"}, {}, "IN_PROTOCOL", IMM, {}, {}, (False, "NT", "S", "NT", "F", "NT", "NT", "NOT_EST")),
]


def _row(r):
    f = r["falsifiers"]
    return (r["hGate"],) + tuple(ABBR[x] for x in (r["EP-1"], f["F-PS-01"], f["F-PS-02"], f["F-PS-03"],
                                                   f["F-PS-05"], f["F-PS-06"], r["SI-6"]))


def run_examples():
    return [(vid, _row(evaluate(toy_case(o, s, rt, h, pr, pe))), exp) for vid, o, s, rt, h, pr, pe, exp in EXAMPLES]


TQ = "Who holds final decision authority, and what does the hierarchy operatively do?"
TT = "The CEO retained final authority over integration decisions."
A1 = "final decision authority"
A2 = "what does the hierarchy operatively do"
FA = "The CEO retained final authority"
QT = "retained final authority over integration decisions"


def toy_pkt(slot):
    p = {"caseId": "c", "sideId": "s", "factId": "p", "d": "D-04", "question": TQ, "factText": TT}
    p["packetId"] = pid("MAIN", slot, tuple_key(p))
    return p


P1, P2 = toy_pkt("R1"), toy_pkt("R2")


def rd(rid, out, status, qa=A1, ex=None, gloss=""):
    return {"readingId": rid, "output": out, "questionAnchor": qa, "factAnchor": FA if out == YES else "",
            "gloss": gloss, "status": status, "exclusion": ex}


def fx(out, qa, quote=QT, cls="readings generated by this part of the question"):
    return {"flipClass": cls, "flipOutput": out, "questionAnchor": qa, "criterion": "c", "quote": quote}


def rec(p, readings, fxs, claim=None):
    r = {"packetId": p["packetId"], "readings": readings, "flipExclusions": fxs,
         "state": None, "output": None, "constructNote": None}
    r["state"], r["output"] = claim if claim else derived_state(r, p["question"])
    return r


def yes2(p, gloss=""):
    return rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE", gloss=gloss)], [fx(NO, A2)])


def no2(p):
    return rec(p, [rd("a", NO, "ADMISSIBLE")], [fx(YES, A2)])


def s1r(p):
    return rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE"), rd("b", NO, "ADMISSIBLE", qa=A2)], [])


def s3r(p):
    return rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE"), rd("b", NO, "NOT_EXCLUDED", qa=A2)], [fx(NO, A2)])


def ndr(p):
    return rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "EXCLUDED", ex={"criterion": "a", "quote": A1})], [])


def conf(p, other):
    return {"packetId": p["packetId"], "verdict": "CONFIRM", "endorsements": excl_ids(other, "other"), "challenges": []}


def chal(p, out, qa=A2, fa=""):
    return {"packetId": p["packetId"], "verdict": "CHALLENGE", "endorsements": [],
            "challenges": [{"basis": "UNWRITTEN_CLASS", "target": None,
                            "reading": {"output": out, "questionAnchor": qa, "factAnchor": fa, "gloss": ""}}]}


def label_examples():
    y1, y2 = yes2(P1), yes2(P2)
    n1, n2 = no2(P1), no2(P2)
    bad = rec(P1, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE")], [], claim=("S2", YES))
    badc = {"packetId": P1["packetId"], "verdict": "CONFIRM", "endorsements": [], "challenges": []}
    return [
        ("L01", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, conf(P1, y2), conf(P2, y1)), ("Y", "BLIND_S2_REPRODUCED_UNCHALLENGED")),
        ("L02", assign_label(P1, P2, n1, n2, conf(P1, n2), conf(P2, n1)), ("N", "BLIND_S2_REPRODUCED_UNCHALLENGED")),
        ("L03", assign_label(P1, P2, s1r(P1), s1r(P2)), ("I", "BLIND_S1_REPRODUCED")),
        ("L04", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, n2), ("U-NR", "NOT_REPRODUCED")),
        ("L05", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, conf(P1, y2), chal(P2, NO)), ("U-NR", "DETERMINACY_CHALLENGED")),
        ("L06", assign_label(P1, P2, ndr(P1), y2), ("U-ND", "NOT_DETERMINABLE_FROM_PACKET")),
        ("L07", assign_label(P1, P2, bad, y2), ("U-NX", "BLIND_INCOMPLETE")),
        ("L08", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2), ("U-NX", "CHALLENGE_INCOMPLETE")),
        ("L09", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, badc, conf(P2, y1)), ("U-NX", "CHALLENGE_INCOMPLETE")),
        ("L10", assign_label(P1, P2, s1r(P1), y2), ("U-NR", "NOT_REPRODUCED")),
        ("L11", assign_label(P1, P2, s3r(P1), y2), ("U-NR", "NOT_REPRODUCED")),
        ("L12", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, conf(P1, y2), chal(P2, YES, qa=A1, fa=FA)), ("U-NX", "CHALLENGE_INCOMPLETE")),
    ]


def cx_m02():
    codex_fx = [{"flipClass": "only an unrelated numerical reading", "criterion": "c", "quote": "Who"}]
    lit = [{"packetId": p["packetId"], "readings": [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE")], "flipExclusions": codex_fx,
            "state": "S2", "output": YES, "constructNote": None} for p in (P1, P2)]
    weak1 = [fx(NO, "Who", quote="Who", cls="only an unrelated numerical reading")]
    claimed = [rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE")], weak1, claim=("S2", YES)) for p in (P1, P2)]
    honest = [rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE")], weak1) for p in (P1, P2)]
    weak2 = [fx(NO, A2, quote="do", cls="only an unrelated numerical reading")]
    w = [rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE")], weak2) for p in (P1, P2)]
    return [
        ("CX-M02-1", assign_label(P1, P2, lit[0], lit[1]), ("U-NX", "BLIND_INCOMPLETE")),
        ("CX-M02-2a", assign_label(P1, P2, claimed[0], claimed[1]), ("U-NX", "BLIND_INCOMPLETE")),
        ("CX-M02-2b", assign_label(P1, P2, honest[0], honest[1]), ("U-NR", "NOT_REPRODUCED")),
        ("CX-M02-3", assign_label(P1, P2, w[0], w[1]), ("U-NX", "CHALLENGE_INCOMPLETE")),
        ("CX-M02-4", assign_label(P1, P2, w[0], w[1], conf(P1, w[1]), chal(P2, NO)), ("U-NR", "DETERMINACY_CHALLENGED")),
        ("CX-M02-5", assign_label(P1, P2, w[0], w[1], conf(P1, w[1]), conf(P2, w[0])), ("Y", "BLIND_S2_REPRODUCED_UNCHALLENGED")),
        ("CX-M02-6", assign_label(P1, P2, yes2(P1), no2(P2)), ("U-NR", "NOT_REPRODUCED")),
    ]


def legacy_eg04(obj, ctx):
    keys = {"packetId", "caseId", "sideId", "factId", "factText", "d", "question"}
    if not isinstance(obj, dict) or set(obj) != keys:
        return False
    fk = "|".join((obj["caseId"], obj["sideId"], obj["factId"]))
    return ctx["stage1"].get(fk) == obj["factText"] and ctx["questions"].get(obj["d"]) == obj["question"]


def m03_context():
    qd5 = "How is the core work actually produced?"
    bank = {"Q-T1": {"d": "D-05", "factText": "At Orrin Bakeries every loaf is baked by hand.", "output": YES}}
    ctx = {"slot": "R1", "questions": {"D-04": TQ, "D-05": qd5}, "stage1": {"c|s|p": TT}, "bank": bank,
           "blind": {("R1", tuple_key(P1)): yes2(P1), ("R2", tuple_key(P2)): yes2(P2)},
           "rh": {"c|s|p": ["D-04"]},
           "recorded": {"c|s|p|D-04": recorded_readings("U-NR", yes2(P1), no2(P2))}}
    qp = {"caseId": QUAL_CASE, "sideId": QUAL_SIDE, "factId": "Q-T1", "d": "D-05", "question": qd5,
          "factText": bank["Q-T1"]["factText"]}
    qp["packetId"] = pid("QUAL", "R1", tuple_key(qp))
    ci = {"packetId": P1["packetId"], "packet": P1, "ownRecord": yes2(P1), "otherRecord": strip_pid(yes2(P2)),
          "sharedOutput": YES}
    hi = {"factKey": "c|s|p", "factText": TT,
          "domains": [{"d": "D-04", "question": TQ, "readings": ctx["recorded"]["c|s|p|D-04"]}]}
    return ctx, {"MAIN": P1, "QUAL": qp, "CHAL": ci, "H": hi}


def cx_m03():
    ctx, objs = m03_context()
    phases = ("MAIN", "QUAL", "CHAL", "H")
    matrix = {(a, b): validate_input(b, objs[a], ctx)[0] for a in phases for b in phases}
    legacy = {a: legacy_eg04(objs[a], ctx) for a in phases}
    qrec = rec(objs["QUAL"], [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE", qa="How is the core work actually produced",
                                 gloss="")], [fx(NO, "core work", quote="How")])
    qrec["readings"][0]["factAnchor"] = "every loaf is baked by hand"
    qrec["state"], qrec["output"] = derived_state(qrec, objs["QUAL"]["question"])
    qbad = dict(qrec)
    qbad["flipExclusions"] = []
    qbad["state"], qbad["output"] = derived_state(qbad, objs["QUAL"]["question"])
    hrec = {"factKey": "c|s|p", "determination": "INVARIANT_PRESENT", "factAnchors": [FA],
            "contractAnchors": ["Tree consumer"], "gloss": ""}
    hbad = dict(hrec)
    hbad["contractAnchors"] = ["not in the excerpt"]
    excerpt = "H-2.1 TREE-CONSUMER RELEVANCE TEST: a fact has a Tree consumer iff ..."
    return {
        "matrixDiagonalOnly": all(matrix[(a, b)] == (a == b) for a in phases for b in phases),
        "legacyAcceptsOnlyMain": legacy == {"MAIN": True, "QUAL": False, "CHAL": False, "H": False},
        "qualPass": qual_pass([objs["QUAL"]], [qrec], ctx["bank"]) is True,
        "qualFail": qual_pass([objs["QUAL"]], [qbad], ctx["bank"]) is False,
        "hRecordValid": validate_h_record(hrec, objs["H"], excerpt)[0] is True,
        "hRecordInvalid": validate_h_record(hbad, objs["H"], excerpt)[0] is False,
    }


def cx_m01():
    extra = [("c|s|u%02d|D-01" % i, "NN") for i in range(39)] + [("c|u|v%02d|D-02" % i, "YY") for i in range(5)]
    case = toy_case({}, {}, "EXTERNAL", {}, {}, {}, extra)
    r = evaluate(case)
    cov = r["coverage"]
    ok1 = (r["SI-6"] == SUFF and cov["NN|D-01|c|s"] == {"state": "EVALUATED", "required": True, "population": 41, "examined": 2}
           and cov["YY|D-02|c|u"] == {"state": "NOT_ESTABLISHED", "required": False, "population": 5, "examined": 0})
    bad = dict(case)
    bad["labels"] = dict(case["labels"])
    bad["labels"]["c|s|u00|D-01"] = "Y"
    try:
        evaluate(bad)
        ok2 = False
    except ValueError:
        ok2 = True
    return {"CX-M01-1": ok1, "CX-M01-2": ok2, "CX-M01-3": evaluate(toy_case({}, {}, "EXTERNAL", {}, {}, {}))["SI-6"] == r["SI-6"]}


def cx_minor01():
    n = bad = unresolved_suff = 0
    free = (E + "|D-03", E + "|D-04", E + "|D-05")
    for labs in product(LABELS, repeat=3):
        over = dict(zip(free, labs))
        for route in H_ROUTES:
            for h in ("IMMATERIAL", "MATERIAL", None):
                h21 = {} if h is None else {E: h}
                case = toy_case(over, {}, route, h21, {}, {})
                r = evaluate(case)
                lab, xset = case["labels"], set(case["X"])
                st = ep1(case["clauses"], lab)[1]
                rh = r_h(xset, lab)
                pred = (all(v == "SAT" for v in st.values()) and all(lab[t] != "U-NX" for t in xset)
                        and (not rh or (route == "IN_PROTOCOL" and all(h21.get(f) == "IMMATERIAL" for f in rh))))
                got = r["SI-6"] == SUFF
                n += 1
                bad += got != pred
                if got and any(lab[t] in ("I", "U-NR", "U-ND") for t in free):
                    unresolved_suff += 1
    return n, bad, unresolved_suff


def cx_minor02():
    y1, y2 = yes2(P1), yes2(P2)
    long1 = yes2(P1, gloss="x" * 301)
    seven = rec(P1, [rd("r%d" % i, YES, "ADMISSIBLE") for i in range(7)], [fx(NO, A2)])
    return [
        ("CX-m02-0", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, conf(P1, y2), conf(P2, y1))[0], "Y"),
        ("CX-m02-1", assign_label(P1, P2, long1, y2, conf(P1, y2), conf(P2, long1))[0], "U-NX"),
        ("CX-m02-2", assign_label(P1, P2, seven, y2)[0], "U-NX"),
        ("CX-m02-3", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, conf(P1, y2), None)[0], "U-NX"),
    ]


def label_property_check():
    def recs(p):
        return {"YES2": yes2(p), "NO2": no2(p), "S1": s1r(p), "S3": s3r(p), "ND": ndr(p), "NONE": None,
                "BAD": rec(p, [rd("a", YES, "ADMISSIBLE")], [], claim=("S2", YES))}

    def mk(kind, p, own, other):
        if kind == "NONE" or own is None or other is None:
            return None
        if kind == "CONF":
            return conf(p, other)
        if kind == "CHAL":
            return chal(p, NO) if own.get("output") == YES else chal(p, YES, qa=A1, fa=FA)
        return {"packetId": p["packetId"], "verdict": "CONFIRM", "endorsements": [], "challenges": []}
    n = bad = 0
    ra, rb = recs(P1), recs(P2)
    for k1, k2 in product(ra, rb):
        r1, r2 = ra[k1], rb[k2]
        for c1k, c2k in product(("NONE", "CONF", "CHAL", "BADC"), repeat=2):
            c1, c2 = mk(c1k, P1, r1, r2), mk(c2k, P2, r2, r1)
            lab, basis = assign_label(P1, P2, r1, r2, c1, c2)
            n += 1
            v = r1 is not None and r2 is not None and validate_blind(r1, P1)[0] and validate_blind(r2, P2)[0]
            s1 = derived_state(r1, TQ) if v else None
            s2 = derived_state(r2, TQ) if v else None
            if lab in ("Y", "N"):
                ok = (v and s1[0] == "S2" and s1 == s2 and c1 is not None and c2 is not None
                      and validate_chal(c1, P1, r1, r2, s1[1]) == (True, "") and c1["verdict"] == "CONFIRM"
                      and validate_chal(c2, P2, r2, r1, s1[1]) == (True, "") and c2["verdict"] == "CONFIRM")
            elif lab == "I":
                ok = v and s1[0] == "S1" and s2[0] == "S1"
            else:
                ok = True
            bad += not ok
    return n, bad


def materiality_check(maxlen=4):
    n = bad = 0
    for req in ("Y", "N"):
        for v in LABELS:
            c = {"id": "LIT:x", "type": "LIT", "tuples": ["x"], "req": req}
            n += 1
            bad += label_materiality(c, {"x": v}) != general_label_materiality(c, {"x": v})
    for k in range(1, maxlen + 1):
        ts = ["t%d" % i for i in range(k)]
        c = {"id": "FREE:x", "type": "FREE", "tuples": ts, "attr": []}
        for vs in product(LABELS, repeat=k):
            lab = dict(zip(ts, vs))
            n += 1
            bad += label_materiality(c, lab) != general_label_materiality(c, lab)
    return n, bad


def si6_exhaustive():
    names = ["F-PS-0%d" % i for i in range(1, 8)]
    pk = ("P1", "P2", "P5", "P7", "P8", "P9")
    n = suff = bad = 0
    for fs in product((SUR, FIRED, NT, ND), repeat=7):
        f = dict(zip(names, fs))
        for ps in product((True, False), repeat=6):
            out = si6(f, dict(zip(pk, ps)))[0]
            n += 1
            ok_all = all(x == SUR for x in fs) and all(ps)
            suff += out == SUFF
            bad += (out == SUFF) != ok_all
    return n, suff, bad


def cx_c1f01():
    y1, y2 = yes2(P1), yes2(P2)
    ok = conf(P1, y2)

    def ch(basis="UNWRITTEN_CLASS", target=None, out=NO, qa=A2, fa="", gloss="", n=1, en=()):
        return {"packetId": P1["packetId"], "verdict": "CHALLENGE", "endorsements": list(en),
                "challenges": [{"basis": basis, "target": target,
                                "reading": {"output": out, "questionAnchor": qa, "factAnchor": fa, "gloss": gloss}}] * n}
    nn1, nn2 = no2(P1), no2(P2)
    v = lambda cr, own=y1, oth=y2, sh=YES: validate_chal(cr, P1, own, oth, sh)[0]
    return [
        ("CX-C1F01-1", v(ok), True),
        ("CX-C1F01-2", v(dict(ok, endorsements=ok["endorsements"] * 2)), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-3", v(dict(ok, endorsements=[])), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-4", v(dict(ok, endorsements=["own:fx:0"])), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-5", v(dict(ok, challenges=ch()["challenges"])), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-6", v(ch()), True),
        ("CX-C1F01-7", v(ch(n=0)), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-8", v(ch(n=7)), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-9", v(ch(out=YES, qa=A1, fa=FA)), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-10", v(ch(gloss="x" * 300)), True),
        ("CX-C1F01-11", v(ch(gloss="x" * 301)), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-12", v(ch(qa="not in the question")), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-13", v(ch(fa="not in the fact")), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-14", v(ch(out=YES, qa=A1, fa=""), nn1, nn2, NO), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-15", v(ch(out=YES, qa=A1, fa=FA), nn1, nn2, NO), True),
        ("CX-C1F01-16", v(ch(basis="WRONG_EXCLUSION", target="own:fx:0")), True),
        ("CX-C1F01-17", v(ch(basis="WRONG_EXCLUSION", target="other:fx:9")), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-18", v(ch(basis="UNWRITTEN_CLASS", target="other:fx:0")), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-19", v(ch(en=["other:fx:0"])), True),
        ("CX-C1F01-20", v(ch(en=["other:fx:0", "other:fx:0"])), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-21", v(dict(ok, packetId=P2["packetId"])), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-22", v(dict(ok, note="")), False),
        ("CX-C1F01-23", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, ok, conf(P2, y1))[0], "Y"),
        ("CX-C1F01-24", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, ch(gloss="x" * 301), conf(P2, y1))[0], "U-NX"),
        ("CX-C1F01-25", assign_label(P1, P2, y1, y2, ch(), conf(P2, y1))[0], "U-NR"),
    ]


def cx_c1f02():
    side = "toy-audit|ORG"
    fa, fb, fc, fd = (side + "|F-" + x + "|D-01" for x in "ABCD")
    cells = {"YY|D-01|" + side: [fa, fd], "NN|D-01|" + side: [fb],
             "YN|D-01|" + side: [fc], "NY|D-01|" + side: []}
    xset = {fa, fc}
    exp = {"YY|D-01|" + side: {"state": "EVALUATED", "required": True, "population": 2, "examined": 1},
           "NN|D-01|" + side: {"state": "NOT_ESTABLISHED", "required": False, "population": 1, "examined": 0},
           "YN|D-01|" + side: {"state": "NOT_ESTABLISHED", "required": True, "population": 1, "examined": 1},
           "NY|D-01|" + side: {"state": "STRUCTURALLY_EMPTY", "required": False, "population": 0, "examined": 0}}
    a = fps03(cells, xset, {fa: "Y", fc: "U-NX"})
    exp_ok = dict(exp)
    exp_ok["YN|D-01|" + side] = dict(exp["YN|D-01|" + side], state="EVALUATED")
    b = fps03(cells, xset, {fa: "Y", fc: "N"})
    probe = {"NN|D-01|" + side: [], "YY|D-04|" + side: [side + "|F-A|D-04"]}
    plab = {side + "|F-A|D-04": "Y"}
    closed = dict(probe)
    for s in STRATA:
        for d in ("D-01", "D-04"):
            closed.setdefault(s + "|" + d + "|" + side, [])
    c = fps03(closed, set(plab), plab)
    missing = {k: v for k, v in cells.items() if k != "NY|D-01|" + side}
    wrong_d = dict(cells, **{"NN|D-01|" + side: [side + "|F-B|D-02"]})
    dup = dict(cells, **{"NN|D-01|" + side: [fb, fa]})
    orphan = dict(cells, **{"YN|D-01|" + side: []})
    r = evaluate(toy_case({}, {}, "EXTERNAL", {}, {}, {}))
    cov = r["coverage"]
    full = (len(cov) == 4 * len(TOY_D) * 2
            and all(set(v) == {"state", "required", "population", "examined"} for v in cov.values())
            and all((v["state"] == "STRUCTURALLY_EMPTY") == (v["population"] == 0) for v in cov.values())
            and all(v["required"] == (v["examined"] > 0) for v in cov.values())
            and sum(v["examined"] for v in cov.values()) == len(TOY_F) * len(TOY_D))
    return {
        "CX-C1F02-1": a == (FIRED, exp),
        "CX-C1F02-2": b == (SUR, exp_ok),
        "CX-C1F02-3": fps03(probe, set(plab), plab) == (NT, {}),
        "CX-C1F02-4": c[0] == SUR and c[1]["NN|D-01|" + side] == exp["NY|D-01|" + side] and len(c[1]) == 8,
        "CX-C1F02-5": fps03(missing, xset, {fa: "Y", fc: "N"}) == (NT, {}),
        "CX-C1F02-6": fps03(wrong_d, xset, {fa: "Y", fc: "N"}) == (NT, {}),
        "CX-C1F02-7": fps03(dup, xset, {fa: "Y", fc: "N"}) == (NT, {}),
        "CX-C1F02-8": fps03(orphan, xset, {fa: "Y", fc: "N"}) == (NT, {}),
        "CX-C1F02-9": full and r["SI-6"] == SUFF,
    }


def run_all():
    ex = run_examples()
    le = label_examples()
    m02 = cx_m02()
    m03 = cx_m03()
    m01 = cx_m01()
    mi1 = cx_minor01()
    mi2 = cx_minor02()
    lp = label_property_check()
    mc = materiality_check()
    s6 = si6_exhaustive()
    f01 = cx_c1f01()
    f02 = cx_c1f02()
    summary = {
        "examples": [vid for vid, got, exp in ex if got != exp],
        "labelExamples": [vid for vid, got, exp in le if got != exp],
        "cxM02": [vid for vid, got, exp in m02 if got != exp],
        "cxM03": [k for k, v in m03.items() if not v],
        "cxM01": [k for k, v in m01.items() if not v],
        "cxMinor01": mi1,
        "cxMinor02": [vid for vid, got, exp in mi2 if got != exp],
        "labelProperty": lp,
        "materiality": mc,
        "si6": s6,
        "cxC1F01": [vid for vid, got, exp in f01 if got != exp],
        "cxC1F02": [k for k, v in f02.items() if not v],
    }
    ok = (not summary["examples"] and not summary["labelExamples"] and not summary["cxM02"]
          and not summary["cxM03"] and not summary["cxM01"] and mi1 == (1296, 0, 54)
          and not summary["cxMinor02"] and lp[1] == 0 and mc[1] == 0 and s6 == (1048576, 1, 0)
          and not summary["cxC1F01"] and not summary["cxC1F02"])
    return ok, summary
```
